st.step, \"state\": st.state, \"detail\": st.detail} for st in job.steps],\n149|        )\n150|\n151|\n152|@router.get(\"/agent-runtimes/{runtime_id}\")\n153|def get_agent_runtime(runtime_id: str, ident: ServiceIdentity = Depends(_auth(\"runtime:read\"))):\n154|    Session = get_session_factory(\"arm\")\n155|    with Session() as session:\n156|        r = session.get(AgentRuntime, uuid.UUID(runtime_id))\n157|        if r is None:\n158|            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"runtime not found\")\n159|        return _runtime_out(r)\n160|\n161|\n162|@router.get(\"/agent-runtimes\")\n163|def list_agent_runtimes(ident: ServiceIdentity = Depends(_auth(\"runtime:read\")), acms_agent_id: str | None = None):\n164|    Session = get_session_factory(\"arm\")\n165|    with Session() as session:\n166|        q = select(AgentRuntime)\n167|        if acms_agent_id:\n168|            q = q.where(AgentRuntime.acms_agent_id == acms_agent_id)\n169|        rows = session.execute(q.order_by(AgentRuntime.created_at.desc())).scalars().all()\n170|        return {\"runtimes\": [_runtime_out(r).model_dump() for r in rows], \"count\": len(rows)}\n171|\n172|\n173|@router.post(\"/agent-runtimes/{runtime_id}/desired-state\")\n174|def set_desired_state(runtime_id: str, body: DesiredStateRequest, ident: ServiceIdentity = Depends(_auth(\"runtime:write\"))):\n175|    Session = get_session_factory(\"arm\")\n176|    with Session() as session:\n177|        r = session.get(AgentRuntime, uuid.UUID(runtime_id))\n178|        if r is None:\n179|            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"runtime not found\")\n180|        try:\n181|            r.desired_state = RuntimeState(body.desired_state)\n182|        except ValueError:\n183|            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=f\"unknown desired state {body.desired_state}\")\n184|        session.add(RuntimeEvent(actor=ident.service, action=\"desired_state_set\", runtime_id=r.runtime_id,\n185|                                 detail={\"desired\": body.desired_state, \"reason\": body.reason}))\n186|        session.commit()\n187|        return _runtime_out(r)\n188|\n189|\n190|@router.delete(\"/agent-runtimes/{runtime_id}\", status_code=200)\n191|def destroy_agent_runtime(runtime_id: str, ident: ServiceIdentity = Depends(_auth(\"runtime:destroy\"))):\n192|    \"\"\"Destroy a Runtime-Manager-owned runtime VM (§8). NEVER deletes ACMS\n193|    persistent agent identity/history.\"\"\"\n194|    s = __import__(\"server_manager.common.config.settings\", fromlist=[\"get_settings\"]).get_settings()\n195|    Session = get_session_factory(\"arm\")\n196|    provider = ProxmoxVMProvider()\n197|    with Session() as session:\n198|        r = session.get(AgentRuntime, uuid.UUID(runtime_id))\n199|        if r is None:\n200|            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"runtime not found\")\n201|        if r.vmid is None or r.node is None:\n202|            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=\"runtime has no backing VM\")\n203|        try:\n204|            verify_ownership(provider, r.node, r.vmid, r, s.protected_vmids)\n205|        except OwnershipError as e:\n206|            session.add(RuntimeEvent(actor=ident.service, action=\"destroy_refused\", runtime_id=r.runtime_id,\n207|                                     result=\"refused\", detail={\"reason\": str(e)}))\n208|            session.commit()\n209|            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=f\"destroy refused: {e}\")\n210|        # audit BEFORE the destructive act (§8 order)\n211|        session.add(RuntimeEvent(actor=ident.service, action=\"destroy_audit\", runtime_id=r.runtime_id,\n212|                                 detail={\"vmid\": r.vmid, \"node\": r.node}))\n213|        session.commit()\n214|        provider.shutdown(r.node, r.vmid)\n215|        provider.destroy(r.node, r.vmid)\n216|        r.actual_state = ActualState.DESTROYED\n217|        r.desired_state = RuntimeState.DESIRED_DESTROYED\n218|        r.vmid = None\n219|        r.node = None\n220|        session.add(RuntimeEvent(actor=ident.service, action=\"destroy_complete\", runtime_id=r.runtime_id,\n221|                                 detail={\"note\": \"ACMS persistent identity/history NOT deleted\"}))\n222|        session.commit()\n223|        return {\"status\": \"DESTROYED\", \"runtime_id\": runtime_id,\n224|                \"note\": \"runtime destroyed; ACMS persistent identity/history preserved\"}\n225|\n226|\n227|@router.get(\"/capabilities\")\n228|def capabilities(ident: ServiceIdentity = Depends(_auth(\"health:read\"))):\n229|    return {\n230|        \"api\": \"server-manager\", \"api_contract_version\": API_CONTRACT_VERSION,\n231|        \"endpoints\": [\n232|            \"POST /api/v1/agent-runtimes\", \"GET /api/v1/provisioning-jobs/{job_id}\",\n233|            \"GET /api/v1/agent-runtimes/{runtime_id}\", \"GET /api/v1/agent-runtimes\",\n234|            \"POST /api/v1/agent-runtimes/{runtime_id}/desired-state\",\n235|            \"DELETE /api/v1/agent-runtimes/{runtime_id}\",\n236|            \"GET /api/v1/model-routes/{route}\", \"GET /api/v1/usage\",\n237|        ],\n238|        \"provider\": \"proxmox_vm\",\n239|        \"destroy_policy\": \"ownership-verified Runtime-Manager-created VMs only (§8)\",\n240|    }", "total_lines": 239, "file_size": 10273, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"content": "1|\"\"\"Proxmox VM provider — runtime-provider abstraction (§8) with DESTRUCTIVE\n2|permissions gated behind ownership verification.\n3|\n4|Hard rules (§8):\n5|- May create/destroy VMs ONLY when the VM was created/claimed by ARM and\n6|  carries durable ownership metadata proving it.\n7|- NEVER destroy: STEA-004/VM906, pre-existing VMs, model-serving VMs,\n8|  infrastructure VMs, or any VM whose ownership marker cannot be verified.\n9|- Before destructive deletion: verify ownership → verify not protected →\n10|  preserve state per policy → snapshot if required → audit → clean stop →\n11|  destroy VM + disposable storage.\n12|\"\"\"\n13|from __future__ import annotations\n14|\n15|import json\n16|import time\n17|import urllib.parse\n18|import urllib.request\n19|import urllib.error\n20|import ssl\n21|from dataclasses import dataclass, field\n22|from typing import Any\n23|\n24|from server_manager.agent_runtime_manager.models.orm import OWNERSHIP_REQUIRED_KEYS, AgentRuntime\n25|from server_manager.common.config.settings import get_settings\n26|\n27|\n28|class OwnershipError(Exception):\n29|    \"\"\"Raised when a destructive action targets a VM without verified ARM ownership.\"\"\"\n30|\n31|\n32|@dataclass\n33|class VMSpec:\n34|    name: str\n35|    node: str\n36|    template_vmid: int\n37|    template_node: str\n38|    bridge: str = \"vmbr0\"\n39|    ciuser: str = \"stagent\"\n40|    ip_mode: str = \"dhcp\"\n41|    description_meta: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n42|\n43|\n44|class ProxmoxVMProvider:\n45|    \"\"\"Thin, allowlisted Proxmox API client for runtime lifecycle only.\"\"\"\n46|\n47|    def __init__(self, api_url: str | None = None, token_id: str | None = None, token_secret: str | None = None,\n48|                 verify_tls: bool = False):\n49|        s = get_settings()\n50|        self.api_url = (api_url or s.pve_api_url or os_env(\"SERVER_MANAGER_PVE_URL\", \"\")).rstrip(\"/\")\n51|        self.token_id = token_id or s.pve_token_id or os_env(\"SERVER_MANAGER_PVE_TOKEN_ID\", \"\")\n52|        self.token_secret = (token_secret or os_env(\"SERVER_MANAGER_PVE_TOKEN_SECRET\", \"\")\n53|                             or _read_pve_secret())\n54|        self.verify_tls = verify_tls\n55|        self._ctx = ssl.create_default_context()\n56|        if not verify_tls:\n57|            self._ctx.check_hostname = False\n58|            self._ctx.verify_mode = ssl.CERT_NONE\n59|\n60|    # ------------------------------------------------------------- transport\n61|    def _call(self, method: str, path: str, body: dict | None = None, timeout: int = 30) -> Any:\n62|        url = f\"{self.api_url}{path}\"\n63|        data = None\n64|        headers = {\"Authorization\": f\"PVEAPIToken={self.token_id}={self.token_secret}\"}\n65|        if body is not None:\n66|            data = urllib.parse.urlencode(body).encode()\n67|            headers[\"Content-Type\"] = \"application/x-www-form-urlencoded\"\n68|        req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, headers=headers, method=method)\n69|        try:\n70|            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout, context=self._ctx) as resp:\n71|                payload = json.loads(resp.read().decode() or \"{}\")\n72|        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:\n73|            detail = e.read().decode()[:300]\n74|            raise RuntimeError(f\"PVE {method} {path} -> HTTP {e.code}: {detail}\") from e\n75|        return payload.get(\"data\", payload)\n76|\n77|    # new_vmid, \"name\": spec.name[:63], \"full\": 1, \"target\": spec.template_node}, timeout=600)\n84|\n85|    def wait_clone_lock_release(self, node: str, vmid: int, max_s: int = 240) -> None:\n86|        \"\"\"Wait for the post-clone lock release.\n87|\n88|        During the clone the config read FAILS (PVE 500 'VM is locked (clone)') —\n89|        a failed read is NOT lock-free; keep waiting (found live: the configure\n90|        step raced the clone and orphaned a VM).\n91|        \"\"\"\n92|        deadline = time.time() + max_s\n93|        saw_config = False\n94|        while time.time() < deadline:\n95|            try:\n96|                cfg = self._call(\"GET\", f\"/nodes/{node}/qemu/{vmid}/config\") or {}\n97|                saw_config = True\n98|            except Exception:\n99|                saw_config = False\n100|                cfg = {}\n101|            if saw_config and not cfg.get(\"lock\"):\n102|                return\n103|            time.sleep(3)\n104|        raise RuntimeError(f\"clone lock still held on {node}/{vmid} after {max_s}s\")\n105|\n106|    def configure_cloud_init(self, spec: VMSpec, vmid: int, extra: dict | None = None,\n107|                             retries: int = 3) -> None:\n108|        # Lock guard: refuse to configure a locked VM (clone race guard, found live)\n109|        for _ in range(retries):\n110|            try:\n111| STILL LOCKED\n114|            if not cfg.get(\"lock\"):\n115|                break\n116|            time.sleep(3)\n117|        else:\n118|            raise RuntimeError(f\"VM {vmid} still locked before configure\")\n119|        body = {\n120|            \"net0\": f\"virtio,bridge={spec.bridge}\",\n121|            \"ipconfig0\": f\"ip={spec.ip_mode}\",\n122|            \"ciuser\": f\"/nodes/{node}/qemu/{vmid}/status/start\", {}, timeout=120)\n131|\n132|    def shutdown(self, node: str, vmid: int, timeout_s: int = 180) -> None:\n133|        try:\n134| self.stop_force(node, vmid)  # §8: clean stop where possible, escalate otherwise\n138|\n139|    def stop_force(self, node: str, vmid: int) -> None:\n140|        self._call(\"POST\", f\"/nodes/{node}/qemu/{vmid}/status/stop\", {}, timeout=120)\n141|\n142|    def destroy(self, node: str, vmid: int, purge: bool = True, destroy_unreferenced: bool = True) -> None:\n143|        body: dict[str, Any] = {}\n144|        if purge:\n145|            body[\"purge\"] = 1\n146|        if destroy_unreferenced:\n147|            body[\"destroy-unreferenced-disks\"] = 1\n148|        self._call(\"DELETE\", f\"/nodes/{node}/qemu/{vmid}\", body or None, timeout=300)\n149|\n150|    # ------------------------------------------------------------- reads\n151|    def vm_config(self, use.\"\"\"\n193|    import os\n194|\n195|    path = os.environ.get(\"SERVER_MANAGER_PVE_SECRET_FILE\", \"/etc/llm-manager/secrets/pve_token\")\n196|    try:\n197|        with open(path) as f:\n198|            return f.read().strip()\n199|    except OSError:\n200|        return \"\"\n201|\n202|\n203|# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n204|# OWNERSHIP VERIFICATION (§8) — the safety core\n205|# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n206|\n207|def verify_ownership(provider: ProxmoxVMProvider, node: str, vmid: int, runtime: AgentRuntime,\n208|                     protected_vmids: set[int], require_protection_check: bool = True) -> None:\n209|    \"\"\"Verify a VM is Runtime-Manager-owned before ANY destructive action.\n210|\n211|    Ownership = the VM's PVE description carries the durable marker created at\n212|    provision time AND matches this runtime's identity AND the VMID is not\n213|    protected/pinned.\n214|    \"\"\"\n215|    # 1) protected/pinned check (VM906 etc., §8)\n216|    if require_protection_check and vmid in protected_vmids:\n217|        raise OwnershipError(f\"VMID {vmid} is protected — destruction refused (§8)\")\n218|\n219|    # 2) VM must still exist\n220|    if not provider.vm_exists(node, vmid):\n221|        raise OwnershipError(f\"VM {node}/{vmid} does not {runtime.runtime_id} is not marked created_by_agent_runtime_manager\")\n226|\n227|    # 4) durable on-VM ownership marker must match\n228|    desc = provider.vm_config(node, vmid).get(\"description\") marker\")\n233|\n234|    if not marker.get(\"created_by_agent_runtime_manager\"):\n235|        raise OwnershipError(f\"VM {node}/{vmid} marker lacks created_by_agent_runtime_manager\")\n236|    if must be complete (§8 minimum set)\n240|    missing = [k for k in OWNERSHIP_REQUIRED_KEYS if k not in marker and k != \"created_by_agent_runtime_manager\"]\n241|    if missing:\n242|        raise {"content": "1|\"\"\"Provisioning service — the ARM state machine (§8/§9/§15/§16).\n2|\n3|Sequenced steps, each recorded in provisioning_steps; idempotent on\n4|request_id; writes durable ownership metadata on the VM at create time;\n5|bootstraps Hermes + Agent Bridge; records hermes_state branch metadata.\n6|\"\"\"\n7|from __future__ import annotations\n8|\n9|import json\n10|import logging\n11|import secrets as pysecrets\n12|import uuid\n13|from dataclasses import dataclass\n14|from datetime import datetime, timezone\n15|\n16|from sqlalchemy import select\n17|from sqlalchemy.orm import Session as SASession\n18|\n19|from server_manager.agent_runtime_manager.models.orm import (\n20|    ActualState,\n21|    AgentRuntime,\n22|    BridgeRegistration,\n23|    JobState,\n24|    ProvisioningJob,\n25|    ProvisioningStep,\n26|    RuntimeEvent,\n27|    RuntimeState,\n28|)\n29|from server_manager.agent_runtime_manager.providers.proxmox_vm import (\n30|    ProxmoxVMProvider,\n31|    VMSpec,\n32|    ownership_marker,\n33|)\n34|from server_manager.common.config.settings import get_settings\n35|\n36|log = logging.getLogger(\"arm.provisioning\")\n37|\n38|\n39|@dataclass\n40|class ProvisionRequest:\n41|    acms_agent_id: str\n42|    request_id: str\n43|    name: str\n44|    harness: str = \"hermes\"\n45|    runtime_class: str = \"software_development_worker\"\n46|    site: str = \"marion-ia-usa\"\n47|    model_route: str | None = None\n48|    bridge_profile: dict | None = None\n49|    desired_state: RuntimeState = RuntimeState.DESIRED_RUNNING\n50|    # template selection (defaults to the proven Hermes golden template)\n51|    template_vmid: int = 121\n52|    template_node: str = \"miam00111\"\n53|\n54|\n55|class ProvisioningService:\n56|    def __init__(self, provider: ProxmoxVMProvider | None = None):\n57|        self.provider = provider or ProxmoxVMProvider()\n58|\n59|    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ api\n60|    def submit(self, session: SASession, req: ProvisionRequest, requested_by: str, authority: str) -> tuple[ProvisioningJob, bool]:\n61|        \"\"\"Idempotent submit: same request_id → the original job (§10/§13).\"\"\"\n62|        existing = session.execute(\n63|            select(ProvisioningJob).where(ProvisioningJob.request_id == req.request_id)\n64|        ).scalar_one_or_none()\n65|        if existing is not None:\n66|            return existing, False\n67|\n68|        runtime = AgentRuntime(\n69|            acms_agent_id=req.acms_agent_id,\n70|            provisioning_request_id=req.request_id,\n71|            name=req.name,\n72|            harness=req.harness,\n73|            runtime_class=req.runtime_class,\n74|            site=req.site,\n75|            model_route=req.model_route,\n76|            desired_state=req.desired_state,\n77|            actual_state=ActualState.ABSENT,\n78|            created_by_agent_runtime_manager=True,  # ARM-created VMs carry ownership markers (§8)\n79|            template_source=f\"{req.template_node}/qemu/{req.template_vmid}\",\n80|            ownership_meta={\"bridge_profile\": req.bridge_profile or {}},\n81|        )\n82|        session.add(runtime)\n83|        session.flush()  # assign runtime.runtime_id (Python-side default) before FK use\n84|        job = ProvisioningJob(\n85|            request_id=req.request_id,\n86|            runtime_id=runtime.runtime_id,\n87|            requested_by=requested_by,\n88|            authority=authority,\n89|            state=JobState.QUEUED,\n90|        )\n91|        session.add(job)\n92|        session.add(RuntimeEvent(actor=requested_by, action=\"provision_submitted\", runtime_id=runtime.runtime_id,\n93|                                 detail={\"request_id\": req.request_id, \"authority\": authority}))\n94|        session.commit()\n95|        return job, True\n96|\n97|    def run_job(self, session: SASession, job_id: uuid.UUID) -> None:\n98|        \"\"\"Execute a queued job's steps synchronously (worker loop calls this).\"\"\"\n99|        job = session.get(ProvisioningJob, job_id)\n100|        if job is None or job.state not in (JobState.QUEUED,):\n101|            return\n102|        job.state = JobState.RUNNING\n103|        runtime = session.get(AgentRuntime, job.runtime_id)\n104|        session.commit()\n105|        try:\n106|            vmid, node = self._provision_vm(session, job, runtime)\n107|            self._bootstrap_hermes(session, job, runtime, node, vmid)\n108|            runtime.actual_state = ActualState.RUNNING\n109|            runtime.vmid = vmid\n110|            runtime.node = node\n111|            job.state = JobState.DONE\n112|            session.add(RuntimeEvent(actor=\"arm\", action=\"provision_complete\", runtime_id=runtime.runtime_id,\n113|                                     detail={\"vmid\": vmid, \"node\": node}))\n114|            session.commit()\n115|        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — job failure is a recorded state, not a crash\n116|            log.exception(\"provisioning job %s failed\", job_id)\n117|            job.state = JobState.FAILED\n118|            job.error = f\"{e.__class__.__name__}: {e}\"[:800]\n119|            runtime.actual_state = ActualState.ERROR\n120|            session.add(RuntimeEvent(actor=\"arm\", action=\"provision_failed\", runtime_id=runtime.runtime_id,\n121|                                     result=\"error\", detail={\"error\": job.error}))\n122|            session.commit()\n123|\n124|    # -------------------------------------------------------------- steps\n125|    def _step(self, session: SASession, job: ProvisioningJob, seq: int, step: str, state: str = \"RUNNING\",\n126|              detail: str | None = None) -> None:\n127|        # A step is recorded twice per sequence number (start + terminal update).\n128|        # Upsert on (job_id, seq) so the terminal update replaces the RUNNING row\n129|        # instead of violating uq_provstep_job_seq.\n130|        existing = session.execute(\n131|            select(ProvisioningStep).where(\n132|                ProvisioningStep.job_id == job.job_id, ProvisioningStep.seq == seq\n133|            )\n134|        ).scalar_one_or_none()\n135|        if existing is not None:\n136|            existing.step = step\n137|            existing.state = state\n138|            existing.detail = detail\n139|        else:\n140|            session.add(ProvisioningStep(job_id=job.job_id, seq=seq, step=step,\n141|                                         state=state, detail=detail))\n142|        session.commit()\n143|\n144|\n145|    def _provision_vm(self, session: SASession, job: ProvisioningJob, runtime: AgentRuntime) -> tuple[int, str]:\n146|        s = get_settings()\n147|        self._step(session, job, 1, \"VALIDATING\")\n148|        if runtime.acms_agent_id in (\"\", None):\n149|            raise ValueError(\"acms_agent_id required (§9)\")\n150|        self._step(session, job, 1, \"VALIDATING\", \"DONE\")\n151|        self._step(session, job, 2, \"RESERVING\", \"DONE\")\n152|        self._step(session, job, 3, \"CLONING_VM\")\n153|        vmid = self.provider.next_vmid()\n154|        node = \"miam00111\"  # template host — the clone targets this node\n155|        spec = VMSpec(\n156|            name=runtime.name,\n157|            node=node,\n158|            template_vmid=121,\n159|            template_node=node,\n160|            description_meta=ownership_marker(runtime, node, vmid, runtime.template_source or \"miam00111/qemu/121\"),\n161|        )\n162|        self.provider.clone_template(spec, vmid)\n163|        self.provider.wait_clone_lock_release(node, vmid)\n164|        session.add(RuntimeEvent(actor=\"arm\", action=\"vm_cloned\", runtime_id=runtime.runtime_id,\n165|                                 detail={\"vmid\": vmid, \"node\": node, \"template\": spec.template_vmid}))\n166|        self._step(session, job, 3, \"CLONING_VM\", \"DONE\")\n167|\n168|        self._step(session, job, 4, \"CONFIGURING_CLOUD_INIT\")\n169|        self.provider.configure_cloud_init(spec, vmid)\n170|        self._step(session, job, 4, \"CONFIGURING_CLOUD_INIT\", \"DONE\")\n171|\n172|        self._step(session, job, 5, \"BOOTING\")\n173|        self.provider.start(node, vmid)\n174|        ip = self.provider.wait_for_ip(node, vmid, max_s=420)\n175|        if not ip:\n176|            raise RuntimeError(\"no guest IP after boot (420s)\")\n177|        runtime.ownership_meta[\"ip\"] = ip\n178|        self._step(session, job, 5, \"BOOTING\", \"DONE\", detail=ip)\n179|        return vmid, node\n180|\n181|    def _bootstrap_hermes(self, session: SASession, job: ProvisioningJob, runtime: AgentRuntime,\n182|                          node: str, vmid: int) -> None:\n183|        \"\"\"§9/§10A: Hermes profile init + Agent Bridge credential + Git branch backup.\n184|\n185|        Bridge bootstrap is executed over SSH from the ARM host using the\n186|        provisioning key (the same delivery path the proven AgentManager\n187|        vertical slice used). Bridge credential lives in ARM DB only.\n188|        \"\"\"\n189|        self._step(session, job, 6, \"WAITING_GUEST\", \"DONE\")\n190|\n191|        # bridge credential (stored hashed/last-4 only in metadata; raw value delivered once)\n192|        bridge_token = pysecrets.token_urlsafe(24)\n193|        runtime.ownership_meta[\"bridge_credential_id\"] = f\"bridge-{runtime.runtime_id.hex[:12]}\"\n194|        runtime.ownership_meta[\"bridge_token_last4\"] = bridge_token[-4:]\n195|\n196|        self._step(session, job, 7, \"INJECTING_BRIDGE_CONFIG\")\n197|        runtime.ownership_meta[\"bridge_bootstrap\"] = {\n198|            \"status\": \"deferred_to_step8\",\n199|            \"injected_at\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n200|        }\n201|        session.add(BridgeRegistration(\n202|            runtime_id=runtime.runtime_id,\n203|            acms_agent_id=runtime.acms_agent_id,\n204|            bridge_credential_id=runtime.ownership_meta[\"bridge_credential_id\"],\n205|        ))\n206|        self._step(session, job, 7, \"INJECTING_BRIDGE_CONFIG\", \"DONE\")\n207|\n208|        # §10A state backup fields are filled by the hermes_bootstrap module\n209|        self._step(session, job, 8, \"HERMES_STATE_BRANCH\")\n210|        runtime.hermes_state_repo = \"startupteams/hermes-base-setup\"\n211|        runtime.hermes_state_branch = f\"agent/{runtime.acms_agent_id[:8]}\"\n212|        runtime.state_sync_health = \"PENDING\"\n213|        self._step(session, job, 8, \"HERMES_STATE_BRANCH\", \"DONE\", detail=runtime.hermes_state_branch)\n214|", "total_lines": 213, "file_size": 9474, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

Prod endpoints are\n5|documented in deploy/production-manifest.yaml, never hard-coded as defaults\n6|(plan §8, proven by PR #20).\n7|\"\"\"\n8|from __future__ import annotations\n9|\n10|import os\n11|from dataclasses import dataclass, field\n12|from functools import lru_cache\n13|\n14|\n15|def _from_secrets_file(path: str, keys: list[str]) -> dict[str, str]:\n16|    vals: dict[str, str] = {}\n17|    try:\n18|        with open(path) as f:\n19|            for line in f:\n20|                line = line.strip()\n21|                if \"=\" in line and not line.startswith(\"#\"):\n22|                    k, v = line.split(\"=\", 1)\n23|                    if k in keys:\n24|                        vals[k] = v\n25|    except OSError:\n26|        pass\n27|    return vals\n28|\n29|\n30|@dataclass\n31|class DbSettings:\n32|    \"\"\"Connection settings for one logical database (§1: separate DBs per service).\"\"\"\n33|\n34|    name: str\n35|    host: str\n36|    port: int\n37|    db: str\n38|    user: str\n39|    password: str\n40|    url: str  # {"output": "\"\"\"SQLAlchemy 2.x engine/session factories for the Server Manager databases.\n\nTwo logical databases (§1): llmmanager (existing) + agent_runtime_manager (new).\nBoth may share the same PG host; credentials are separate; no cross-database FKs.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom sqlalchemy import create_engine, event\nfrom sqlalchemy.engine import Engine\nfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker\n\nfrom server_manager.common.config.settings import get_settings\n\n_engines: dict[str, Engine] = {}\n_sessions: dict[str, sessionmaker] = {}\n\n\ndef _make_url(dsn_host: str, port: int, db: str, user: str, password: str) -> str:\n    from urllib.parse import quote_plus\n\n    return f\"postgresql+psycopg2://{quote_plus(user)}:{quote_plus(password)}@{dsn_host}:{port}/{db}\"\n\n\ndef get_engine(db: str = \"arm\") -> Engine:\n    \"\"\"db='arm' → agent_runtime_manager DB; db='llm' → llmmanager DB.\"\"\"\n    if db in _engines:\n        return _engines[db]\n    s = get_settings()\n    if db == \"arm\":\n        url = _make_url(s.arm_pg_host, s.arm_pg_port, s.arm_pg_db, s.arm_pg_user, s.arm_pg_password)\n    elif db == \"llm\":\n        url = _make_url(s.llm_pg_host, s.llm_pg_port, s.llm_pg_db, s.llm_pg_user, s.llm_pg_password)\n    elif db == \"litellm\":\n        # LiteLLM proxy's own DB (SpendLogs). Same PG host, same app role (pg_app_creds),\n        # separate logical database per the existing app's own dual-DSN pattern (main.py:609).\n        url = _make_url(s.llm_pg_host, s.llm_pg_port, \"litellm\", s.llm_pg_user, s.llm_pg_password)\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(f\"unknown logical db {db!r} (use 'arm', 'llm', or 'litellm')\")\n    eng = create_engine(url, pool_pre_ping=True, pool_size=5, max_overflow=5, future=True)\n    _engines[db] = eng\n    return eng\n\n\ndef get_session_factory(db: str = \"arm\") -> sessionmaker:\n    if db in _sessions:\n        return _sessions[db]\n    _sessions[db] = sessionmaker(bind=get_engine(db), expire_on_commit=False, future=True)\n    return _sessions[db]\n\n\ndef session_scope(db: str = \"arm\") -> Session:\n    \"\"\"Context-manager session: commit on success, rollback on error, always close.\"\"\"\n    factory = get_session_factory(db)\n    return factory()===\n__init__.py\n__pycache__\nsession.py", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

{"output": "bootstrap-vm.sh\nbuild-release.sh\ndeploy-release.sh\nhealthcheck.sh\npreflight.sh\nproduction-manifest.yaml\nREADME.md\nrollback.sh\nverify-parity.sh\n---\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# build-release.sh — build an immutable LLM Manager release artifact from a Git worktree.\n# Usage: build-release.sh [worktree-dir] [output-dir]\n# Produces: llm-manager-<git-sha>.tar.gz + llm-manager-<git-sha>.tar.gz.sha256\n# Excludes: secrets, venvs, logs, DB dumps/contents, caches, .git\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSRC_DIR=\"${1:-$(pwd)}\"\nOUT_DIR=\"${2:-$(pwd)/dist}\"\n\nGIT_SHA=\"$(git -C \"$SRC_DIR\" rev-parse HEAD)\"\nGIT_SHA_SHORT=\"$(git -C \"$SRC_DIR\" rev-parse --short=12 HEAD)\"\nBUILD_TIME=\"$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)\"\n\necho \"==> Building release for ${GIT_SHA} from ${SRC_DIR}\"\nWORK=\"$(mktemp -d)\"\ntrap 'rm -rf \"$WORK\"' EXIT\nSTAGE=\"$WORK/llm-manager-${GIT_SHA_SHORT}\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$STAGE\"\n# Copy tracked files only — this is the strongest guarantee that the artifact\n# equals reviewed source (untracked local junk can never leak in).\ngit -C \"$SRC_DIR\" archive --format=tar HEAD | tar -x -C \"$STAGE\"\n\n# Release manifest (non-secret build identity)\npython3 - \"$STAGE\" \"$GIT_SHA\" \"$BUILD_TIME\" <<'PY'\nimport hashlib, json, os, sys\nstage, sha, build_time = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]\nfiles = {}\nfor root, dirs, names in os.walk(stage):\n    dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in (\".git\", \"__pycache__\", \".venv\", \"venv\")]\n    for n in sorted(names):\n        p = os.path.join(root, n)\n        rel = os.path.relpath(p, stage)\n        h = hashlib.sha256(open(p, \"rb\").read()).hexdigest()\n        files[rel] = h\nmanifest = {\n    \"git_sha\": sha,\n    \"build_time\": build_time,\n    \"python\": \"3.12\",", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

{"status": "success", "output": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# deploy-release.sh — the LLM Manager release transaction (plan §13).\n# Sequence: preflight -> snapshot -> suppress recovery -> install candidate ->\n# candidate validation -> migrations -> activate -> restart (recovery LAST) -> smoke\n# -> commit, or automatic application rollback on smoke failure (plan §14).\n# Usage: deploy-release.sh <release-tarball> [--staging] [--skip-migrations]\nset -uo pipefail\n\nTARBALL=\"${1:?usage: deploy-release.sh <release-tarball> [--staging] [--skip-migrations]}\"; shift || true\nSTAGING=0; SKIP_MIG=0\nfor a in \"$@\"; do\n  [ \"$a\" = \"--staging\" ] && STAGING=1\n  [ \"$a\" = \"--skip-migrations\" ] && SKIP_MIG=1\ndone\n\nAPP_ROOT=/opt/llm-manager\nRELEASES=$APP_ROOT/releases\nLEDGER=/var/log/llm-manager/release-ledger.jsonl\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LEDGER\")\" \"$APP_ROOT/shared/logs\" \"$APP_ROOT/shared/state\"\n\nstamp() { date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ; }\nledger() { echo \"{\\\"ts\\\":\\\"$(stamp)\\\",\\\"event\\\":\\\"$1\\\",\\\"detail\\\":$2}\" >> \"$LEDGER\"; }\n\ndie() { echo \"FATAL: $*\" >&2; ledger \"abort\" \"\\\"$*\\\"\"; exit 1; }\n\necho \"==> LLM Manager release transaction ($(stamp))\"\n\n# ---- Phase A: preflight --------------------------------------------------------\n# First promotion of the flat layout (prod, no current symlink yet): allow the\n# missing-current preflight check as a WARN for this one transaction.\nPROMOTION=0\nif [ \"$STAGING\" = 0 ] && [ ! -e \"$APP_ROOT/current\" ]; then\n    PROMOTION=1\n    export LLM_MANAGER_FIRST_PROMOTION=1\nfi\n\"$(dirname \"$0\")/preflight.sh\" \"$TARBALL\" $([ \"$STAGING\" = 1 ] && echo --staging) || die \"preflight failed\"\n\n# snapshot current state\nPREV_REL=\"\"\n[ -e \"$APP_ROOT/current\" ] && PREV_REL=\"$(basename \"$(readlink -f \"$APP_ROOT/current\")\")\"\nPREV_HEALTH=\"$(curl -sk --max-time 5 http://127.0.0.1/healthz 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nledger \"snapshot\" \"{\\\"previous_release\\\":\\\"$PREV_REL\\\",\\\"health\\\":$(echo \"${PREV_HEALTH:-null}\" | python3 -c 'import sys,json; s=sys.stdin.read().strip(); print(s if s.startswith(\"{\") else \"null\")')}\"\necho \"==> snapshot: previous release ${PREV_REL:-none}\"\n\n# ---- Phase B: unpack + verify artifact -----------------------------------------\nSTAGE=\"$(mktemp -d)\"\ntar -xzf \"$TARBALL\" -C \"$STAGE\"\nREL_SRC=\"$(find \"$STAGE\" -maxdepth 1 -type d -name 'llm-manager-*' | head -1)\"\n[ -n \"$REL_SRC\" ] || die \"unexpected artifact layout\"\nREL_SHA=\"$(python3 -c \"import json;print(json.load(open('$REL_SRC/release_manifest.json'))['git_sha'])\")\"\nREL_DST=\"$RELEASES/$REL_SHA\"\n\n# verify manifest checksums of what we unpacked\npython3 - \"$REL_SRC\" <<'PY' || die \"artifact manifest verification failed\"\nimport hashlib, json, os, sys\nstage = sys.argv[1]\nman = json.load(open(os.path.join(stage, \"release_manifest.json\")))\nbad = []\nfor rel, expect in man[\"files\"].items():\n    p = os.path.join(stage, rel)\n\n", "tool_calls_made": 0, "duration_seconds": 0.03}

{"content": "1|\"\"\"Server Manager client for ACMS (JINT-001 paired work, REV4 §12/§13).\n2|\n3|ACMS reserves the persistent agent identity (its own agents table) and uses\n4|this client to request runtime provisioning from Server Manager. ACMS never\n5|touches Proxmox/VMID details beyond optional diagnostics (§13).\n6|\n7|Contract pinned: Server Manager API v1.0.0 (agent-runtimes + provisioning-jobs).\n8|\"\"\"\n9|from __future__ import annotations\n10|\n11|import json\n12|import urllib.error\n13|import urllib.request\n14|from dataclasses import dataclass\n15|from uuid import uuid4\n16|\n17|API_CONTRACT_VERSION = \"1.0.0\"\n18|\n19|\n20|class ServerManagerError(Exception):\n21|    \"\"\"Transport/protocol failure talking to Server Manager.\"\"\"\n22|\n23|\n24|class ServerManagerConflict(ServerManagerError):\n25|    \"\"\"409 from Server Manager (e.g. destroy without backing VM).\"\"\"\n26|\n27|\n28|class ServerManagerForbidden(ServerManagerError):\n29|    \"\"\"403 — includes ownership-guard refusals (§8).\"\"\"\n30|\n31|\n32|@dataclass\n33|class RuntimeHandle:\n34|    runtime_id: str\n35|    acms_agent_id: str\n36|    actual_state: str\n37|    vmid: int | None\n38|    node: str | None\n39|    hermes_state_branch: str | None\n40|    raw: dict\n41|\n42|    @classmethod\n43|    def from_api(cls, d: dict) -> \"RuntimeHandle\":\n44|        return cls(\n45|            runtime_id=d[\"runtime_id\"], acms_agent_id=d[\"acms_agent_id\"],\n46|            actual_state=d[\"actual_state\"], vmid=d.get(\"vmid\"), node=d.get(\"node\"),\n47|            hermes_state_branch=d.get(\"hermes_state_branch\"), raw=d,\n48|        )\n49|\n50|\n51|@dataclass\n52|class ProvisionJob:\n53|    job_id: str\n54|    state: str\n55|    error: str | None\n56|    steps: list[dict]\n57|    created: bool\n58|\n59|    @classmethod\n60|    def from_api(cls, d: dict) -> \"ProvisionJob\":\n61|        return cls(job_id=d[\"job\"][\"job_id\"], state=d[\"job\"][\"state\"],\n62|                   error=d[\"job\"].get(\"error\"), steps=d[\"job\"].get(\"steps\", []),\n63|                   created=d.get(\"created\", False))\n64|\n65|\n66|class ServerManagerClient:\n67|    \"\"\"Minimal, dependency-free client (urllib, like acms/bridge.py pattern).\"\"\"\n68|\n69|    def __init__(self, base_url: str, token: str, timeout: int = 30):\n70|        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip(\"/\")\n71|        self.token = token\n72|        self.timeout = timeout\n73|\n74|    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- transport\n75|    def _call(self, method: str, path: str, body: dict | None = None,\n76|              expected: tuple[int, ...] = (200, 201, 202), timeout: int | None = None) -> dict:\n77|        data = json.dumps(body).encode() if body is not None else None\n78|        req = urllib.request.Request(f\"{self.base_url}{path}\", data=data, method=method)\n79|        req.add_header(\"Authorization\", f\"Bearer {self.token}\")\n80|        if body is not None:\n81|            req.add_header(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n82|        try:\n83|            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout or self.timeout) as resp:\n84|                return json.loads(resp.read().decode() or \"{}\")\n85|        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:\n86|            detail = e.read().decode(errors=\"replace\")[:400]\n87|            if e.code == 403:\n88|                raise ServerManagerForbidden(f\"{e.code} {detail}\") from None\n89|            if e.code in (409,):\n90|                raise ServerManagerConflict(f\"{e.code} {detail}\") from None\n91|            raise ServerManagerError(f\"{e.code} {detail}\") from None\n92|        except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError) as e:\n93|            raise ServerManagerError(str(e)) from None\n94|\n95|    # ------------------------------------------------------------------- api v1\n96|    def create_runtime(self, acms_agent_id: str, name: str, harness: str = \"hermes\",\n97|                       runtime_class: str = \"software_development_worker\",\n98|                       model_route: str | None = None,\n99|                       bridge_profile: dict | None = None,\n100|                       request_id: str | None = None) -> ProvisionJob:\n101|        \"\"\"POST /api/v1/agent-runtimes — idempotent on request_id (§10/§13).\n102|\n103|        Provisions synchronously server-side (clone+boot can take minutes), so\n104|        this call uses a long timeout (600s) — found live: the 30s default\n105|        timed out while the job completed successfully server-side.\n106|        \"\"\"\n107|        body = {\n108|            \"acms_agent_id\": {"output": "\"\"\"Background scheduling for live telemetry (combined slice 3+4; ADR-0010; plan §16).\n\nLightweight in-process loop — no Celery/K8s/event bus. Runs inside the FastAPI\nlifespan; every DB operation gets its own session so the loop never holds a\nrequest-scoped session. Time is injected (``now_fn``) so tests never wait real\nminutes/hours (plan §20).\n\nJobs:\n- stale evaluation (every tick): derive UNKNOWN/HEALTHY/STALE/UNREACHABLE\n- 1-hour stale reconciliation: actively request a full status snapshot\n- 24 h fleet reconciliation (staggered tolerance documented in ADR-0010)\n- telemetry sampling every ACMS_TELEMETRY_SAMPLE_SECONDS + on material change\n\nReconciliation authority (plan §9): correct operational telemetry, preserve\nhistory, log discrepancy events; never touch approved scope/assignment.\nMulti-replica note: a single ACMS instance is assumed; replicas would need\nleader/lock coordination (documented in ADR-0010 + FUTURE_WORK).\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nimport logging\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n\nfrom sqlalchemy import select\nfrom sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n\nfrom .db import SessionLocal\nfrom .telemetry_models import (\n    AgentEventRecord,\n    AgentStatusCurrentRecord,\n    AgentTelemetrySampleRecord,\n    CONNECTIVITY_HEALTHY,\n    CONNECTIVITY_STALE,\n    CONNECTIVITY_UNREACHABLE,\n    CONTACT_SOURCE_RECONCILIATION,\n)\nfrom .telemetry_service import TelemetryService, _new_id, add_event, _now\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"acms.telemetry.scheduler\")\n\n\nclass TelemetryScheduler:\n    def __init__(self, session_factory=None, now_fn=None):\n        self._session_factory = session_factory or SessionLocal\n        self._now_fn = now_fn or _now\n        self._task: asyncio.Task | None = None\n        self._last_fleet_reconcile: datetime | None = None\n        self._last_sample: dict[str, datetime] = {}\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ loop\n\n    def start(self) -> None:\n        if self._task is None or self._task.done():\n            self._task = asyncio.create_task(self._run(), name=\"acms-telemetry-scheduler\")\n    async def stop(self) -> None:\n        if self._task is not None:\n            self._task.cancel()\n            try:\n                await self._task\n            except asyncio.CancelledError:\n                pass\n            self._task = None\n\n    async def _run(self) -> None:\n        from .settings import get_settings\n\n        while True:\n            try:\n                s = get_settings()\n                await self.tick(s)\n            except asyncio.CancelledError:\n                raise\n            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — scheduler must survive transient DB issues\n                logger.exception(\"telemetry scheduler tick failed\")\n            await asyncio.sleep(5)\n    async def tick(self, s) -> None:\n        \"\"\"One scheduler pass. Public so tests can drive time deterministically.\"\"\"\n        now_dt = self._now_fn()", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

lc\n101|\n102|        new_connectivity = status.connectivity\n103|        if delta <= stale_after:\n104|            new_connectivity = CONNECTIVITY_HEALTHY\n105|        elif delta >= reconcile_after:\n106|            ok = await self._attempt_reconciliation(db, s, status, now_dt)\n107|            new_connectivity = CONNECTIVITY_HEALTHY if ok else CONNECTIVITY_UNREACHABLE\n108|        else:\n109|            new_connectivity = CONNECTIVITY_STALE\n110|\n111|        if new_connectivity != status.connectivity:\n112|            prev = status.connectivity\n113|            status.connectivity = new_connectivity\n114|            if new_connectivity == CONNECTIVITY_STALE and status.stale_since is None:\n115|                status.stale_since = now_dt\n116|            elif new_connectivity == CONNECTIVITY_HEALTHY:\n117|                status.stale_since = None\n118|            status.updated_at = now_dt\n119|            await add_event(\n120|                db,\n121|                event_type=\"CONNECTIVITY_CHANGED\", actor_source=\"scheduler\", agent_id=agent_id,\n122|                summary=f\"Connectivity {prev} -> {new_connectivity} after {int(delta.total_seconds())}s without contact\",\n123|            )\n124|        await db.commit()\n125|\n126|        # periodic telemetry sample (plan §8)\n127|        last = self._last_sample.get(agent_id)\n128|        if last is None or (now_dt - last).total_seconds() >= s.telemetry_sample_seconds:\n129|            await self._write_sample(db, status, now_dt)\n130|            self._last_sample[agent_id] = now_dt\n131|\n132|    async def _attempt_reconciliation(self, db: AsyncSession, s, status: AgentStatusCurrentRecord, now_dt: datetime) -> bool:\n133|        \"\"\"Request a full status snapshot from the bridge (1 h stale path).\n134|\n135|        Bridge transport lands in Phase 4; until then reconciliation of an\n136|        agent without a live bridge fails (→ UNREACHABLE), which is the honest\n137|        outcome: we cannot reach it.\"\"\"\n138|        await add_event(db, event_type=\"RECONCILIATION_STARTED\",\n139|            actor_source=\"scheduler\", agent_id=status.agent_id,\n140|            summary=f\"Stale >= {s.stale_reconcile_seconds}s - requesting status snapshot\")\n141|        await db.commit()\n142|        try:\n143|            from .bridge import get_bridge_for_agent  # Phase 4 module\n144|\n145|            bridge = get_bridge_for_agent(status.agent_id)\n146|            snapshot = await bridge.fetch_status()\n147|            await TelemetryService.ingest_heartbeat(db, status.agent_id, snapshot)\n148|            await add_event(db, event_type=\"RECONCILIATION_SUCCEEDED\",\n149|                actor_source=\"reconciliation\", agent_id=status.agent_id,\n150|                summary=\"Reconciliation snapshot ingested; connectivity restored\")\n151|            await db.commit()\n152|            return True\n153|        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — no bridge / transport failure = unreachable\n154|            await add_event(db, event_type=\"RECONCILIATION_FAILED\",\n155|                actor_source=\"reconciliation\", agent_id=status.agent_id,\n156|                summary=f\"Reconciliation failed: {type(exc).__name__}\",\n157|                metadata={\"error\": str(exc)[:300]})\n158|            await db.commit()\n159|            return False\n160|\n161|    async def _maybe_fleet_reconcile(self, db: AsyncSession, s, now_dt: datetime) -> None:\n162|        if self._last_fleet_reconcile is None:\n163|            self._last_fleet_reconcile = now_dt\n164|            return\n165|        if (now_dt - self._last_fleet_reconcile).total_seconds() < s.fleet_reconcile_seconds:\n166|            return\n167|        self._last_fleet_reconcile = now_dt\n168|        logger.info(\"fleet reconciliation tick at %s\", now_dt.isoformat())\n169|        # Phase 4: per-agent reconciliation attempts for agents with bridges.\n170|        # Management scope is never touched here (ADR-0010).\n171|\n172|    async def _write_sample(self, db: AsyncSession, status: AgentStatusCurrentRecord, now_dt: datetime) -> None:\n173|        db.add(AgentTelemetrySampleRecord(\n174|            sample_id=_new_id(), agent_id=status.agent_id, sampled_at=now_dt,\n175|            agent_running=status.agent_running, connectivity=status.connectivity,\n176|            model_id=status.model_id,\n177|            context_used_tokens=status.context_used_tokens,\n178|            context_max_tokens=status.context_max_tokens,\n179|            context_utilization_percent=status.context_utilization_percent,\n180|            context_warning=status.context_warning,\n181|            session_id=status.session_id, session_title=status.session_title,\n182|            session_total_tokens=status.session_total_tokens,\n183|            cumulative_api_tokens=status.cumulative_api_tokens,\n184|        ))\n185|        await db.commit()\n186|", "total_lines": 185, "file_size": 8374, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

idempotency key after a\ntimeout (the caller decides; idempotency replays the original job);\nstructured exceptions; correlation metadata passthrough; credentials from\nexternal configuration only (never hard-coded).\n\nUsage:\n    c = PduManagerClient(\"https://10.0.20.156/api/v1\", user=\"svc-acms\",\n                         password=..., cafile=\"/path/ca.pem\")\n    c.health()\n    plan = ssl\nimport time\nimport urllib.error\nimport urllib.request\n\n\nclass PduError(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Transport/protocol error (includes HTTP status when present).\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, message: str, status_code: int | None = None, body: str | None = None):\n        super().__init__(message)\n        self.status_code = status_code\n        self.body = body\n\n\nclass PduIdentityMismatch(PduError):\n    \"\"\"409 TARGET_IDENTITY_MISMATCH — the wrong-target guard fired (never dispatch).\"\"\"\n\n\nclass PduProtectedPolicy(PduError):\n    \"\"\"Protected-outlet policy refusal (PROTECTED_OFF_FORBIDDEN etc.).\"\"\"\n\n\nclass PduAuth(PduError):\n    \"\"\"401/403 from the API.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass PduManagerClient:\n    def __init__(self, base_url: str, user: str, password: str,\n                 cafile: str | None = None, verify: bool | str = True, timeout: int = 30):\n        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip(\"/\")\n        self._auth = base64.b64encode(f\"{user}:{password}\".encode()).decode()\n        self.timeout = timeout\n        if verify is False:\n            # explicit opt-out ONLY (plan rule: no verify=False default)\n {"output": "# TDR-0008 — llmmanager DB ORM migration is staged, not complete\n\nStatus: Accepted (REV4 §6 allows staged/behavior-preserving migration)\n\n## Context\n\nThe llmmanager database has 20+ public tables + an agentmanager schema, all created by raw\nSQL (db/schema/schema.sql) and accessed by psycopg2 in the 2,600-line single-file app.\nMigrating all of it to SQLAlchemy ORM in one sprint risks regressions in the revenue-critical\nLLM serving path.\n\n## Decision\n\n- This sprint migrates ONLY the new agent_runtime_manager database to ORM+Alembic (greenfield).\n- LLM Manager machine API reads (model-routes, usage) go through SQLAlchemy Core `text()`\n  queries against the EXISTING schema — no schema change, no ORM models for those tables yet.\n- Existing write paths stay on the proven psycopg2 app code.\n- Follow-up sprints migrate tables group by group (deployments/hosts/model_registry first,\n  telemetry second) behind the adapter layer, each with row-count + checksum verification per REV4 §6.\n\n## Consequences\n\n- Two persistence styles coexist temporarily in the llmmanager DB (raw SQL + Core reads).\n- `server_manager.llm_manager.adapters` isolates the eventual cutover.\nschema.sql", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

{"status": "success", "output": "# db/migrations — LLM Manager database migration ledger\n\nNumbered, forward-only SQL migrations with a ledger table (`schema_migrations`:\nversion, checksum, applied_at, git_sha).\n\n- `schema.sql` stays the clean-install reference (all 33 tables), NOT an in-place tool.\n- `migrate.py apply` applies pending migrations in order and records the ledger.\n- Files named `NNN_destructive_*.sql` are refused by ordinary runs — they require a\n  maintenance plan, an explicit `--i-know-this-is-destructive`, an operator-taken backup,\n  and a documented recovery path (plan §8).\n- Before ANY production migration the operator takes a protected local DB backup.\n  Production DB dumps never leave the host and never enter GitHub artifacts.\n- Rollback compatibility: application rollback assumes backward-compatible\n  (expand-only) migrations; destructive ones block ordinary CD rollback by policy.\n---\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"migrate.py — ledger-based forward-only DB migration runner for LLM Manager.\n\nLedger table: schema_migrations(version TEXT PK, checksum TEXT, applied_at TIMESTAMPTZ, git_sha TEXT).\n\nUsage:\n  migrate.py status                     # show applied + pending\n  migrate.py apply  [--git-sha SHA]     # apply pending migrations in order\n  migrate.py verify                     # verify checksums of applied migrations\n\nRules (plan §8):\n- forward-only; destructive migrations are NEVER auto-applied (file must be\n  named NNN_destructive_*.sql and the runner refuses them outside --i-know-this-is-destructive)\n- never uploads/backups anywhere: backups are an operator action pre-migration\n\"\"\"\nimport hashlib\nimport os\nimport sys\n\nimport psycopg2\n\nMIGRATIONS_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\n\n# Connection inputs come from env only — never from code or files committed here.\n# LLM_MANAGER_PG_DSN      full DSN, OR the four vars below\n# LLM_MANAGER_PG_HOST / _DB / _USER / _PASSWORD\n\n\ndef connect():\n    dsn = os.environ.get(\"LLM_MANAGER_PG_DSN\")\n    if dsn:\n        return psycopg2.connect(dsn, connect_timeout=5)\n    # Same credential contract as the app (main.py): env overrides > the\n    # PG_HOST/PG_DB/PG_USER/PG_PW lines inside pg_app_creds > local defaults.\n    # Keeping one source of truth means the transaction needs no duplicated\n    # secret env plumbing (plan §8).\n    file_vals = {}\n    pw_file = os.environ.get(\n        \"LLM_MANAGER_PG_PW_FILE\", \"/etc/llm-manager/secrets/pg_app_creds\")\n    try:\n\n", "tool_calls_made": 0, "duration_seconds": 0.02}

{"status": "success", "output": "\"\"\"Adapters re-exporting the existing proven LLM Manager modules (§5 strangler).\n\nThe legacy import paths (service/app/*) remain the live implementations.\nEach adapter here re-exports it so new code imports via server_manager.*;\nwhen a module is later moved, only these adapters change.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport importlib\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n_REPO_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))))\n_APP_DIR = os.path.join(_REPO_ROOT, \"service\", \"app\")\n\n\ndef _reexport(module_name: str, names: list[str]) -> object:\n    mod = importlib.import_module(module_name)\n    return mod\n\n\ndef _ensure_app_on_path() -> None:\n    if _APP_DIR not in sys.path:\n        sys.path.insert(0, _APP_DIR)\n\n\n_ensure_app_on_path()\n\n# Lazy adapter objects: attribute access resolves into the legacy module so the\n# existing single-file app keeps ownership of its own constants (no import-time\n# side effects beyond what the module already performs today).\nclass _LegacyAdapter:\n    def __init__(self, module_name: str):\n        self._module_name = module_name\n        self._module = None\n\n    def _load(self):\n        if self._module is None:\n            _ensure_app_on_path()\n            self._module = importlib.import_module(self._module_name)\n        return self._module\n\n    def __getattr__(self, name):\n        return getattr(self._load(), name)\n\n\nv011_core = _LegacyAdapter(\"v011_core\")\nactive_hosts = _LegacyAdapter(\"active_deployments\")\nlit\n", "tool_calls_made": 0, "duration_seconds": 0.02}

{"content": "1|\"\"\"Server Manager API v1 — LLM Manager routes (model-routes + usage), §10.\n2|\n3|Machine API (distinct from human UI / A2A). Scoped bearer identity required.\n4|Model routes + usage read from the EXISTING llmmanager DB (strangler: read\n5|only this sprint; writes stay in the proven app).\n6|\"\"\"\n7|from __future__ import annotations\n8|\n9|from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Request\n10|\n11|from server_manager.common.auth.service_tokens import require_identity\n12|from server_manager.common.db.session import get_session_factory\n13|\n14|router = APIRouter(prefix=\"/api/v1\", tags=[\"server-manager-v1-llm\"])\n15|\n16|API_CONTRACT_VERSION = \"1.0.0\"\n17|\n18|\n19|@router.get(\"/model-routes/{route}\")\n20|def get_model_route(route: str, request: Request):\n21|    \"\"\"A model route = a row of model_registry (+ its backends). Read-only.\"\"\"\n22|    require_identity(request, \"route:read\")\n23|    Session = get_session_factory(\"llm\")\n24|    with Session() as session:\n25|        from sqlalchemy import text\n26|\n27|        row = session.execute(\n28|            text(\"SELECT logical_model_name, engine, backend_url, context_limit, health, routable \"\n29|                 \"FROM model_registry WHERE logical_model_name = :m\"),\n30|            {\"m\": route},\n31|        ).mappings().all()\n32|        if not row:\n33|            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f\"no model route {route!r}\")\n34|        return {\"route\": route, \"api_contract_version\": API_CONTRACT_VERSION,\n35|                \"backends\": [dict(r) for r in row]}\n36|\n37|\n38|@router.get(\"/usage\")\n39|def usage(request: Request, hours: int = 24):\n40|    \"\"\"Aggregated token usage from LiteLLM_SpendLogs (read-only, §10).\"\"\"\n41|    require_identity(request, \"usage:read\")\n42|    if hours < 1 or hours > 24 * 30:\n43|        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=\"hours must be 1..720\")\n44|    Session = get_session_factory(\"litellm\")\n45|    with Session() as session:\n46|        from sqlalchemy import text\n47|\n48|        rows = session.execute(text(\n49|            \"\"\"\n50|            SELECT model,\n51|                   count(*)                     AS requests,\n52|                   COALESCE(sum(prompt_tokens),0)     AS prompt_tokens,\n53|                   COALESCE(sum(completion_tokens),0) AS completion_tokens\n54|            FROM \"LiteLLM_SpendLogs\"\n55|            WHERE \"startTime\" >= now() - make_interval(hours => :h)\n56|            GROUP BY model\n57|            ORDER BY requests DESC\n58|            LIMIT 50\n59|            \"\"\"\n60|        ), {\"h\": hours}).mappings().all()\n61|        return {\"window_hours\": hours, \"api_contract_version\": API_CONTRACT_VERSION,\n62|                \"models\": [dict(r) for r in rows]}", "total_lines": 61, "file_size": 2509, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"status": "success", "output": "llm_pg(tmp_path_factory):\n    \"\"\"Embedded PG loaded from schema.sql; sets SERVER_MANAGER_LLM_PG_* env.\"\"\"\n    import pgserver\n\n    data_dir = tmp_path_factory.mktemp(\"llm-parity-pgdata\")\n    with pgserver.get_server(str(data_dir)) as srv:\n        uri = srv.get_uri()  # postgresql://postgres:@/postgres?host=<sockdir>\n        import psycopg2\n\n        from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlencode, urlparse\n\n        u = urlparse(uri)\n        q = parse_qs(u.query)\n        sock_host = q[\"host\"][0]\n        base = f\"host={sock_host} user={u.username or 'postgres'}\"\n        conn = psycopg2.connect(base + \" dbname=postgres\")\n        conn.autocommit = True\n        cur = conn.cursor()\n        cur.execute(\"CREATE DATABASE llmmanager\")\n        cur.execute(\"DO $$ BEGIN IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname='llmmanager') THEN CREATE ROLE llmmanager; END IF; END $$\")\n        cur.close()\n        conn.close()\n        raw = open(os.path.join(REPO, \"db\", \"schema\", \"schema.sql\")).read()\n        # strip pg_dump ≥17 \\restrict/\\unrestrict meta lines (psql-only commands)\n        schema = \"\\n\".join(l for l in raw.splitlines()\n                           if not l.lstrip().startswith(\"\\\\\"))\n        conn = psycopg2.connect(base + \" dbname=llmmanager\")\n        cur = conn.cursor()\n        cur.execute(schema)\n        # seed parity fixtures\n        cur.execute(\"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO hosts (host_id, name, guest_ip, gpu_count, gpu_model, node, vmid,\n                               desired_power_state, management_mode, desired_service_state)\n            VALUES (1, 'MIAM-00111', '10.0.20.168', 6, 'RTX 3080', 'miam00111', 102, 'RUNNING', 'MANAGED', 'SERVING'),\n                   (2, 'MIAM-00143', '10.0.20.163', 6, 'RTX 3080', 'miam00143', 109, 'RUNNING', 'MANAGED', 'SERVING')\n        \"\"\")\n   \n", "tool_calls_made": 0, "duration_seconds": 0.01}

{"output": "583:### ACMS-REQ-053 - Session/work alignment and drift detection\n600:### ACMS-REQ-054 - Context-window telemetry and warning\n- Superseded direction is distinguishable from current direction.\n\n### ACMS-REQ-048 - Goal/context-level progress metrics\n\n**Sprint Priority:** 3\n\n**Requirement**\n\nACMS shall support status/progress metrics at meaningful context levels so humans can evaluate whether product, project, feature, and task-level goals are advancing.\n\n**Acceptance criteria**\n\n- Metrics can be associated with multiple hierarchy levels.\n- The UI distinguishes objective/measured metrics from agent-reported qualitative status.\n\n### ACMS-REQ-049 - Kanban-style work view\n\n**Sprint Priority:** 3\n\n**Requirement**\n\nACMS shall provide a concise work-management view, including a Kanban-style representation or equivalent tagged-ticket view, for planned, active, blocked/review, and completed agent work.\n\n**Acceptance criteria**\n\n- Work items can be filtered by project, agent/manager, status, and priority where available.\n- Human-attention items are visually distinguishable from ordinary active work.\n\n---\n\n## Feature Set: Scalability and Resilience\n\n### ACMS-REQ-050 - Avoid per-action centralized execution dependency\n\n**Sprint Priority:** 3\n\n**Requirement**\n\nACMS architecture shall not require every agent tool invocation or internal execution step to traverse ACMS, so normal agent execution can continue without making ACMS the throughput bottleneck.\n\n**Acceptance criteria**\n\n- Agents execute approved local steps directly inside their authorized environment.\n- ACMS receives significant state/events and exercises governance/control without proxying every low-level action.\n- Loss of the live dashboard stream does not automatically terminate safe local execution already authorized.\n\n### ACMS-REQ-051 - Initial fleet scalability target\n\n**Sprint Priority:** 3\n\n**Requirement**\n\nThe initial ACMS architecture shall support at least 100 concurrently active registered agents on the intended MARION-IA-USA deployment without requiring one browser connection per agent or synchronous polling of every agent for routine status.\n\n**Acceptance criteria**\n\n- Routine status is event/heartbeat driven when available.\n- Browser clients consume aggregated ACMS state/events rather than connecting directly to each agent.\n- Load testing can demonstrate the target before production acceptance.", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

{"status": "success", "output": "### ACMS-REQ-053 - Session/work alignment and drift detection\n\n**Sprint Priority:** 3\n\n**Requirement**\n\nACMS shall compare the agent's reported session/work identity to its approved primary assignment.\n\n**Acceptance criteria**\n\n- Heartbeats contain session ID/title when the harness provides them.\n- ACMS knows the expected work/assignment keys for the active assignment.\n- ACMS derives an alignment of ALIGNED, UNKNOWN, or MISMATCH.\n- Mismatch does not change approved scope.\n- Persistent mismatch while the agent is running can request a structured Markdown handoff after a configurable grace period.\n- Discrepancies are auditable (semantic events).\n\n### ACMS-REQ-054 - Context-window telemetry and warning\n\n**Sprint Priority:** 3\n\n**Requirement**\n\nACMS shall collect available current-context usage telemetry and display used tokens, maximum tokens, and utilization percentage, with configurable warning levels.\n\n**Acceptance criteria**\n\n- Current context-window usage is tracked separately from session/lifetime and cumulative API token counts.\n- Invalid telemetry values (null/negative/unparsable/used > max) are not treated as valid counts and are surfaced as UNKNOWN/INVALID rather than fabricated.\n- Warning levels (ELEVATED/HIGH/CRITICAL thresholds) are configurable and visible.\n- Explicit context-overflow/payload-too-large failures reported by the harness/provider are recorded as significant events.\n\n---\n\n## Feature Set: Security and External-Agent Isolation\n\n### ACMS-REQ-038 - Private internal agent endpoints\n\n**Sprint Priority:** 3\n\n**Requirement**\n\nInternal Agent Bridge endpoints shall be reachable only through approved private network/VPN paths and shall not be intentionally exposed directly to the public Internet in the MVP.\n\n", "tool_calls_made": 0, "duration_seconds": 0.02}

# STEA-004 Autonomous Plane-Flight Execution Plan
## Continue ACMS + Server Manager + Agent Runtime Manager + PDU + Hermes Work After REV4 Handoff

**Date:** 2026-09-27  
**Execution window:** Long autonomous session while human is unavailable (~10-hour flight)  
**Primary agent:** STEA-004 Entrepreneur

## Authority

Continue as far as safely possible inside the already approved architecture.

You are authorized to:

- implement;
- test;
- create PRs;
- self-review;
- merge when repository/release gates pass;
- deploy to staging/production when the existing safe-release gates pass;
- create TDRs and Future Work;
- make ordinary reversible implementation decisions;
- continue from one completed phase to the next without waiting for the human.

Stop only when a true human/product decision is required or a safety/rollback boundary is crossed.

---

# 1. Mission

Do **not** redo completed REV4 work.

Continue by closing the remaining operational gaps and then implementing the newly approved ACMS memory/context-cost architecture.

Highest-value order:

```text
1. Hermes state sync on acms-worker-001
2. ARM desired-state reconciler
3. ACMS runtime lifecycle integration
4. PDU consumer integration
5. full LLM Manager SQLAlchemy/Alembic migration
6. legacy AgentManager decommission/parity
7. ACMS memory/session requirements
8. ACMS Memory/Session Offload MVP
9. per-Work cost/budget controls
10. SSE + Attention/Audit if time remains
```

---

# 2. Current baseline — already proven

Treat the latest HANDOFF as authoritative unless live verification shows a newer compatible state.

Already complete:

```text
Server Manager CI/CD + rollback
credential rotation
server_manager modular package
Agent Runtime Manager DB/API v1
ACMS ServerManagerClient + Create-Agent workflow
persistent ACMS identity reservation
acms-worker-001 = VM124
Hermes bootstrap
Agent Bridge registration/heartbeat
real Work execution
steer
Markdown handoff
VM stop/start
same ACMS identity + same session/history after restart
PDU Manager asset API
PDU identity guard
PDU dry-run/action planning
PDU version/capabilities
PDU correlation audit
PDU Python client
Hermes base repo secret guards
```

Protected resources:

```text
VM906 / STEA-004 Entrepreneur
VM121 template
VM114 Server Manager
CT122 ACMS
VM156 PDU Manager
unowned Proxmox VMs
model-serving infrastructure
```

Do not destroy or repurpose them.

---

# 3. Remaining work from the handoff

Explicitly unfinished:

- ARM desired-state reconciler;
- disposable destroy/recreate validation;
- full LLM Manager ORM migration;
- PDU consumer/service-identity completion;
- per-agent Hermes state sync on VM124;
- ARM/PDU lifecycle correlation;
- legacy AgentManager decommission.

Newly approved architecture from the AI–Human SOP:

- Work may span many disposable execution sessions;
- ACMS stores structured operational memory;
- fresh sessions receive compact selected context;
- handoffs make session rotation safe;
- track current context separately from cumulative API tokens/spend;
- support economic checkpoint/rotation thresholds;
- support per-Work soft/hard cloud budgets;
- send compact semantic summaries upward;
- Executive Agents should eventually reconstruct from ACMS instead of keeping giant perpetual sessions.

---

# 4. Execution-context discipline

This sprint should model the intended workflow.

```text
Human + ChatGPT = WHY
ACMS            = WHAT / WHO / STATUS / COST / RESULT
Git + Markdown  = WHAT CHANGED
Hermes          = HOW for the current bounded task
```

At each PR-sized phase:

```text
finish atomic work
→ tests
→ commit/push
→ concise phase handoff/checkpoint
→ start next phase from compact state
```

Do not keep reloading full historical logs/transcripts.

Cloud context guidance:

```text
<100k tokens       continue
100k–150k          prepare checkpoint
>150k              strongly prefer handoff + fresh context
```

Checkpoint earlier if cumulative API tokens/spend are growing disproportionately.

If `/new` cannot be initiated autonomously, still write a compact phase handoff and avoid pulling old context forward.

---

# 5. Phase 0 — baseline verification

Verify only what is necessary:

- current main SHAs;
- VM114 health/version;
- Server Manager API;
- ARM DB revision;
- ACMS CT122 health/version;
- PDU VM156 health/version;
- VM124 bridge health;
- VM906 untouched.

Do not rerun destructive E2E merely to prove already documented facts.

Create a concise baseline checkpoint.

---

# 6. Phase 1 — finish Hermes state backup on VM124

## Goal

Move `state_sync` from PENDING to verified/healthy.

VM124 should have:

```text
dedicated hermes-base-setup branch
automatic periodic sync
secret scan
successful safe commit/push
observable sync health
```

## Work

1. inspect actual Hermes/profile/state paths on VM124;
2. install agent-specific sync using the existing `hermes-base-setup` mechanism;
3. do not copy VM906 identity/secrets;
4. use existing authorized Git automation credentials from external secret storage;
5. install cron or systemd timer;
6. make one harmless Markdown state change;
7. verify secret scan;
8. commit/push to the dedicated worker branch;
9. record latest state SHA/time/health in ARM if supported;
10. verify persistence across VM restart.

If feasible, add an immediate task-completion state-sync hook. If that creates large coupling, document it and implement a simpler explicit hook first.

Acceptance:

- periodic sync installed;
- manual sync green;
- remote commit visible;
- secret probe rejected;
- no commits to main/master;
- reboot preserves sync;
- backup health is observable or a concrete follow-up is recorded.

Checkpoint.

---

# 7. Phase 2 — implement ARM desired-state reconciler

The API currently records desired state; make it execute safely.

Required behavior:

```text
desired RUNNING
→ observe
→ start owned VM if stopped
→ wait for OS/bridge readiness
→ update actual state

desired STOPPED
→ preserve/checkpoint state as policy requires
→ graceful shutdown
→ verify stopped
→ DO NOT destroy persistent VM

recovery
→ bounded retries/backoff
→ preserve ACMS identity
```

Safety:

- act only on ARM-owned VMs with verified ownership marker;
- ownership mismatch fails closed;
- never touch VM906;
- no Kubernetes work.

Implement:

- reconcile loop;
- lock/single-replica safety;
- retry/backoff;
- `last_reconcile_at`;
- `last_error`;
- restart/recovery count;
- audit;
- desired vs actual state.

Tests:

- no-op when already correct;
- RUNNING starts once;
- STOPPED shuts down once;
- ownership mismatch;
- PVE unavailable;
- slow bridge recovery;
- reconciler restart/idempotency.

Deploy and verify VM124 through one safe API-driven stop/start cycle.

Checkpoint.

---

# 8. Phase 3 — ACMS runtime lifecycle integration

Update ACMS to consume real ARM lifecycle state.

Implement:

- desired runtime state;
- actual runtime state;
- recovery/error state;
- Administrator:
  - Start Runtime
  - Stop Runtime
  - Restart Runtime
  - Reconcile Now
- runtime events in semantic event/audit history.

Keep runtime controls distinct from:

```text
A2A Interrupt
A2A Cancel
Steer
task/session controls
```

If active Work remains assigned while runtime is unavailable, preserve Work state and surface the external runtime dependency.

Do not add casual persistent-worker deletion.

Checkpoint.

---

# 9. Phase 4 — integrate PDU consumers

PDU Manager API hardening is already live.

## Server Manager / ARM

Use the common PDU client for asset-based operations.

Implement high-level functions such as:

```text
get_power_state(asset_id)
plan_power_action(...)
ensure_powered_on(...)
request_reboot(...)
get_power_job(...)
```

Coordinate Proxmox/OS/runtime state around physical power.

Remember:

```text
PDU ON != server ready
```

## ACMS

Implement direct ACMS support for:

- PDU health;
- capabilities;
- asset power state;
- dry-run/action planning;
- job lookup;
- audit/correlation lookup.

Create or reuse an `InfrastructureOperation` durable record.

Correlation should allow:

```text
ACMS Work/Agent
→ infrastructure operation
→ Server Manager operation if applicable
→ PDU job
→ PDU audit
```

Write-capable ACMS methods may be implemented behind Administrator/service authorization.

**Do not perform a real physical ON/OFF/REBOOT during the flight unless the current execution context contains explicit authorization for the exact asset/action.**

Read-only and dry-run validation are allowed.

## LLM Manager

Prefer:

```text
LLM Manager → Server Manager → PDU
```

for compute-node lifecycle.

Checkpoint.

# 10. Phase 5 — continue full LLM Manager SQLAlchemy/Alembic migration

Human direction: full migration remains the target.

First produce a table inventory:

```text
table
owner
authoritative?
row count
read paths
write paths
target SQLAlchemy model
migration status
risk
```

Migrate in domain-sized slices:

1. model registry/logical routes;
2. deployment/revision history;
3. benchmark/qualification;
4. LLM-owned usage/cost metadata;
5. remaining LLM Manager-owned configuration/history;
6. remove legacy psycopg2 paths only after parity.

Do not copy LiteLLM-owned tables just to claim completion; use a defined adapter if LiteLLM remains authoritative.

Rules:

- SQLAlchemy 2.x;
- Alembic for owned schema;
- async where appropriate;
- explicit SQL/Core allowed for complex operations;
- preserve IDs/history;
- validate row counts/content;
- stage before prod;
- backup/rollback before destructive migration;
- no ACMS DB coupling.

Checkpoint after each domain.

projects

because ACMS owns semantic work/context.

# 12. Phase 7 — codify ACMS memory/context/cost requirements

Update ACMS `REQUIREMENTS.md`, `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`, sprint planning, and ADR/TDRs.

Use next available IDs after ACMS-REQ-054.

Recommended approved requirements:

### ACMS-REQ-055 — Work continuity across disposable execution sessions
A Work Item/Program survives across many execution sessions.

### ACMS-REQ-056 — Explicit execution Context Packages
Record the selected context supplied to each session.

### ACMS-REQ-057 — Handoff completeness before rotation
Verify Work ID, status, Git, tests, deployment, decisions, debt, remaining work, next action, artifacts.

### ACMS-REQ-058 — Economic execution-session telemetry
Track separately:
- current context;
- max context;
- utilization;
- cumulative API tokens;
- estimated spend;
- session age;
- context growth rate.

### ACMS-REQ-059 — Work/session cloud budgets
Soft threshold = checkpoint/review/reroute.  
Hard threshold = no new cloud execution without override.

### ACMS-REQ-060 — Relevant-context selection
Select relevant hierarchical context; do not inject everything.

### ACMS-REQ-061 — Compact upward reporting
Prefer semantic summaries + artifact references over full logs/diffs.

### ACMS-REQ-062 — Disposable Executive Agent sessions
Persistent executive identity, fresh reasoning sessions reconstructed from ACMS/handoffs.

Architecture statement:

```text
ChatGPT remembers WHY
ACMS remembers WHAT / WHO / STATUS / COST / RESULT
Git remembers WHAT CHANGED
Hermes temporarily remembers HOW
```

Do not claim ACMS replaces Human+ChatGPT durable design memory.

Commit the documentation slice before implementation.

Checkpoint.

```

Context Package:

source references

policy/version
```

Do not duplicate giant source documents when stable references are sufficient.

Implement API/UI sufficient to:

- list sessions under Work;
- inspect which context package a session received;
- inspect handoff/checkpoint;
- inspect context/cumulative token/spend telemetry.

Add a handoff-completeness validator.

Initially make economic rotation advisory:

```text
GREEN
MONITOR
CHECKPOINT_RECOMMENDED
ROTATE_RECOMMENDED
```

Do not auto-kill active sessions until harness semantics/policy are proven.

Required test:

```text
Work
→ Session 1 + Context Package
→ handoff/checkpoint
→ Session 1 closes
→ Session 2 gets compact reconstructed package
→ same Work remains active
```

This is the primary new ACMS proof from the updated SOP.

# 14. Phase 9 — Work budgets and cost controls

If time remains:

Use authoritative Server/LLM Manager usage metrics.

Add per-Work visibility for:

- cumulative cloud tokens;
- estimated spend;
- model/provider;
- session breakdown;
- soft budget;
- hard budget.

Soft threshold:

- semantic event;
- checkpoint recommendation;
- Attention/advisory;
- consider local reroute.

Hard threshold:

- block **new** cloud execution;
- do not interrupt unsafe atomic work;
- require authorized override.

Never fabricate costs when source data is unavailable.

---

# 15. Phase 10 — SSE + Attention/Audit if time remains

Continue the previously approved ACMS roadmap:

```text
browser SSE semantic-event stream
→ Attention/Audit UI
```

Useful Attention triggers now include:

- UNREACHABLE;
- runtime recovery exhausted;
- critical context/cost threshold;
- failed/rejected execution;
- human-input handoff;
- mandatory approval;
- Hermes state-backup failure.

Ordinary work/session mismatch remains audit + automatic reconciliation first; escalate only if persistent/scope-significant.

---

# 16. Do not guess these product areas while the human is answering architecture questions

Do not implement large speculative behavior for:

- External Agent Gateway customer policy;
- `agentify.me.co` portal product behavior;
- detailed Speed-to-Lead domain/configuration model;
- billing model;
- customer data-retention policy;
- Kubernetes production runtime.

You may:

- update Future Work;
- add Problems to Solve;
- draft Proposed ADRs;
- document interface boundaries already implied by accepted architecture.

Do not mark unresolved product choices Accepted.

---

# 17. Safety and destructive boundaries

Persistent worker policy:

```text
STOP when idle
do not destroy by default
```

Destruction only for:

- explicitly disposable test runtime;
- explicit retirement;
- verified corrupt replacement;
- proven ARM ownership;
- durable state already preserved.

No real PDU hardware actuation without exact asset/action authorization.

No secret values in commits, logs, handoffs, or chat.

---

# 18. TDR discipline

Update/retire:

- LLM Manager ORM migration debt;
- service token → mTLS;
- Git/cron agent-state sync → future Agent State service;
- single-replica reconciler leadership;
- qga long-operation risk;
- legacy AgentManager overlap;
- staging DB parity.

Any new shortcut needs:

```text
why accepted
risk
owner
repayment trigger
```

---

Only genuine architecture/product decisions.

One concise next step.

# 20. Definition of success

A successful flight does not require every phase to finish.

It requires:

- no regression of the proven E2E worker chain;
- meaningful deferred work closed;
- independently verified slices shipped;
- compact checkpoints between phases;
- no protected-resource incident;
- no secret leakage;
- no speculative customer architecture silently accepted;
- exact remaining work documented.

Continue down the priority list until the execution window ends or a real stop condition is reached.
